### What I Implemented

**Task 2: Rewrote the widget PHP bit-dropdown-btn.php v3.0.0**

The complete v3.0.0 implementation was written to:
`/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.php`

### Key Changes v2.0.0 → v3.0.0

1. **MEDIA Control + JetElements Encryption**
   - Replaced simple URL fields (`item_url`) with `MEDIA` control (`item_file`)
   - Added safe JetElements integration: checks both `function_exists()` AND `instanceof Jet_Elements_Download_Handler`
   - Calls `get_download_link()` (the only correct, public method) to generate encrypted URLs
   - Fallback to direct URL when JetElements unavailable

2. **Icon Picker Integration**
   - Added native Elementor `ICONS` control (line 218)
   - Defaults to `eicon-download`, renders via `Icons_Manager::render_icon()`
   - Falls back to custom SVG (circle with down arrow) when picker returns empty
   - Icon size fully configurable (12–64px slider)

3. **Granular Style Controls**
   - Split single "Style" section into 6 dedicated tabs:
     - Button — Dimensions (width, border-radius, padding, box-shadow)
     - Colors — Normal (bg, text, border)
     - Colors — Hover (bg, text, border)
     - Icon (size, color, color hover)
     - Typography (button label + menu items)
     - Menu Dropdown (min-width, item padding, bg, text, border, divider)

4. **Data Structure Changes**
   - Old: `[ 'item_label' => '...', 'item_url' => [ 'url' => '...', 'is_external' => ... ] ]`
   - New: `[ 'item_label' => '...', 'item_file' => [ 'id' => int, 'url' => '...' ], 'item_fallback_url' => [ 'url' => '...', 'is_external' => ... ] ]`

5. **Security & Escaping**
   - All outputs properly escaped: `esc_html()`, `esc_url()`
   - Consistent rel attributes: `rel="nofollow noopener"`
   - Added phpcs:ignore comment for SVG fallback (intentional unescaped output)

That confirms it. Now I have a complete picture. Here is the full review.

---

## Code Review: Task 2 — PHP widget v3.0.0 `bit-dropdown-btn.php`

### What Was Done Well

The implementation is clean and matches the plan spec faithfully. PHP syntax is valid, escaping is properly applied throughout, Elementor patterns are correct, and the section/control structure is well-organised. The `phpcs:ignore` comment on the icon output is appropriately scoped and correctly explains why the suppression is safe (the content comes either from `Icons_Manager::render_icon`, a Wordpress-controlled API, or from the private hardcoded SVG). The CSS variable naming in selectors aligns correctly with the CSS file, including the documented intentional inconsistency `--btn-normal-border-hv`.

---

### Critical Issues (must fix)

**1. The `common/mu-plugins` mirror was NOT updated — it still contains v2.0.0**

The plan requires (Chunk 3, Task 3) that the mirror at `docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.php` be kept in sync. The diff confirms the mirror is still v2.0.0 with completely different logic: it uses `item_url` instead of `item_file`/`item_fallback_url`, has no JetElements integration, has no ICONS control, and has a different SVG method name (`get_download_icon_svg` vs `get_default_icon_svg`). This is a direct violation of the CLAUDE.md rule:

> "Todo mu-plugin criado ou modificado DEVE ser commitado em `common/mu-plugins/`"

File affected: `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.php`

This also means Task 3 (mirror + server-tools commit) has not been executed yet. That task should be blocked on this review but the mirror divergence makes it appear Task 2 itself was committed before Task 3 was run.

---

### Important Issues (should fix)

**2. `$target` is output un-escaped in the anchor tag (line 407)**

```php
echo '<a href="' . $href . '" rel="nofollow noopener"' . $target . '>'
```

`$target` is set to the literal string `' target="_blank"'` or `''`. This is a hardcoded string so there is no actual injection vector, but it violates the defensive escaping pattern. The idiomatic approach is to use a ternary directly into the attribute position with `esc_attr`:

```php
$target_attr = $is_external ? ' target="_blank"' : '';
echo '<a href="' . $href . '" rel="nofollow noopener"' . $target_attr . '>' . $item_label . '</a>';
```

No actual security risk here since the value is never derived from user input, but a static analysis tool (PHPCS) will flag it.

**3. The `ICONS` control is placed inside the Style tab, not the Content tab**

The control `'icon'` (line 226) is registered under `style_icon` with `TAB_STYLE`. In Elementor convention, the icon picker itself is a content control — what *icon* to display is content, while *size and color* are style. Having the picker in the Style tab is non-standard and will confuse editors: they will look in Content for "which icon" and not find it.

The plan does not specify which tab the icon control should go in, so this is a deviation from Elementor best practices rather than from the plan. Recommended fix: move the `icon` control to the Content section, keeping `icon_size`, `color_icn`, and `color_icn_hv` in the Style tab.

**4. Fallback icon SVG uses `fill-rule` without the `-webkit` prefixed path**

The plan (Chunk 2, Step 1) notes that `fill-rule="evenodd"` is necessary for the hollow interior. This is correctly implemented. However, the path data is split across two concatenated string lines (lines 45–46):

```php
. '<path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M11,0C4.92,0,0,4.92,0,11s4.92,11,11,11,11-4.92,11-11'
. 'S17.08,0,11,0Zm.13,8.42h3.5l-1.75,3.03-1.75,3.03-1.75-3.03-1.75-3.03h3.5Z"/>'
```

The path is syntactically valid. The shape described is a circle with an internal chevron-diamond (not a down-arrow as the comment states). Whether this matches the intended design should be verified visually.

---

### Suggestions (nice to have)

**5. No `noreferrer` on `_blank` links**

The current code outputs `rel="nofollow noopener"` for all links (and external-only for the fallback). The security best practice for `target="_blank"` is to include `noreferrer` alongside `noopener` to prevent the referrer header from leaking. Since JetElements-encrypted URLs are internal by nature (they point to `?jet_download=HASH` on the same domain), this only applies to fallback external URLs. Low risk but worth adding for defence in depth.

**6. Empty `dropdown-btn-menu` div rendered when all items have no valid URL**

If all items in the repeater have `attachment_id = 0` and no `fallback_url`, the code enters the `if (!empty($links))` branch, opens `<div class="dropdown-btn-menu">`, iterates and `continue`s every item, then closes the div — rendering an empty menu container in the DOM. This is a minor cosmetic issue but produces invalid empty markup. A pre-filter of the links before opening the menu div would resolve it.

**7. `icon_size` selector overrides the CSS `.dropdown-btn-icon` fixed `width`/`height` with inline styles**

The CSS sets `.dropdown-btn-icon` to `width: 30px; height: 30px`, and the `icon_size` control targets the inner `svg` and `i` elements. The `icon_size` default is also 30px. This layering is correct but could cause visual inconsistency if a user sets `icon_size` to something larger than the container — the icon will overflow the `.dropdown-btn-icon` container since the container's size is not bound to the control. Worth noting for future maintenance: consider binding the container size to the same slider.

---

### Plan Compliance Summary

| Requirement | Status |
|---|---|
| JetElements `get_download_link()` integration | Implemented correctly |
| MEDIA control for attachment | Implemented |
| Fallback URL control | Implemented |
| ICONS control with SVG fallback | Implemented (tab placement debatable) |
| 6 style sections, 20 controls | Implemented — counted 21 controls across 6 sections |
| `instanceof Jet_Elements_Download_Handler` guard | Implemented |
| PHP syntax valid | Confirmed |
| Mirror to `common/mu-plugins/` | NOT DONE — mirror is still v2.0.0 |

---

### Action Required

Before Task 3 can be marked complete, the following must be done:

1. Copy the v3.0.0 file to `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.php`
2. Commit that file in the server-tools repository at `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2`

The two issues classified as Important (icon output escaping and ICONS control tab placement) can be addressed now or deferred to a v3.0.1 patch, but the mirror sync is a blocking defect for this task.

   358→        protected function render() {
   359→            $s     = $this->get_settings_for_display();
   360→            $label = esc_html( ! empty( $s['label'] ) ? $s['label'] : 'Download' );
   361→            $links = ! empty( $s['links'] ) ? $s['links'] : [];
   362→
   363→            echo '<div class="dropdown-btn-wrapper">';
   364→            echo '<div class="dropdown-btn-container">';
   365→            echo '<button class="dropdown-btn-toggle" type="button">';
   366→            echo '<div class="dropdown-btn-content">';
   367→            echo '<span class="dropdown-btn-label">' . $label . '</span>';
   368→
   369→            // Ícone — tenta Icon Picker, cai para SVG padrão
   370→            echo '<span class="dropdown-btn-icon">';
   371→            $icon_settings = $s['icon'] ?? [];
   372→            $icon_html     = '';
   373→            if ( ! empty( $icon_settings['value'] ) ) {
   374→                ob_start();
   375→                \Elementor\Icons_Manager::render_icon( $icon_settings, [ 'aria-hidden' => 'true' ] );
   376→                $icon_html = ob_get_clean();
   377→            }
   378→            echo ! empty( $icon_html ) ? $icon_html : $this->get_default_icon_svg(); // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.EscapeOutput.OutputNotEscaped
   379→            echo '</span>';
   380→
   381→            echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-content
   382→            echo '</button>';
   383→
   384→            // Menu dropdown
   385→            if ( ! empty( $links ) ) {
   386→                echo '<div class="dropdown-btn-menu">';
   387→                foreach ( $links as $link ) {
   388→                    $item_label    = esc_html( $link['item_label'] ?? '' );
   389→                    $attachment_id = (int) ( $link['item_file']['id'] ?? 0 );
   390→                    $fallback_url  = $link['item_fallback_url']['url'] ?? '';
   391→                    $is_external   = ! empty( $link['item_fallback_url']['is_external'] );
   392→
   393→                    if ( $attachment_id > 0
   394→                         && function_exists( 'jet_elements_download_handler' )
   395→                         && jet_elements_download_handler() instanceof Jet_Elements_Download_Handler ) {
   396→                        // Link criptografado via JetElements
   397→                        $href           = esc_url( jet_elements_download_handler()->get_download_link( $attachment_id ) );
   398→                        $target_attr = '';
   399→                    } elseif ( ! empty( $fallback_url ) ) {
   400→                        // URL direta (fallback)
   401→                        $href           = esc_url( $fallback_url );
   402→                        $target_attr = $is_external ? ' target="_blank"' : '';
   403→                    } else {
   404→                        continue; // nenhuma URL válida — não renderiza este item
   405→                    }
   406→
   407→                    echo '<a href="' . $href . '" rel="nofollow noopener"' . $target_attr . '>'
   408→                       . $item_label . '</a>';
   409→                }
   410→                echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-menu
   411→            }
   412→
   413→            echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-container
   414→            echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-wrapper
   415→        }
   416→    }
   417→
   418→    $widgets_manager->register( new Bit_Dropdown_Btn_Widget() );
   419→} );
   420→

## Codebase Exploration Report: Concertacao Amazonia

I've thoroughly explored the WordPress Docker dev environment at `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao`. Here are my key findings:

### 1. WPML String Translation Integration

**Current State:**
- WPML is integrated via **bit-wpml-circle.php** mu-plugin (v1.0.0) which styles the WPML language switcher as circular badges
- Uses the `wpml_home_url` filter hook to return language-aware home URLs
- Example in **bit-elementor-svg-widget.php** (lines 104-107, 276):
  ```php
  : esc_url( apply_filters( 'wpml_home_url', network_home_url( '/' ) ) );
  ```
- The **cmplz-ptbr.php** mu-plugin shows basic translation pattern via `gettext` filter (translates Complianz GDPR labels to Portuguese)
- **No explicit `icl_register_string` or `wpml_translate_single_string` calls found** in mu-plugins or theme

**WPML Pattern Available:**
- The framework supports WPML filters and the infrastructure is multisite-ready
- Multisite setup: blog_id 1 (main), blog_id 2 (cultura subsite) with path-based routing

---

### 2. JetEngine Repeater Field Structure

**Current Implementation - bit-dropdown-btn.php (lines 72-105):**
Uses **Elementor's native Repeater control** (NOT JetEngine repeater):
```php
$repeater = new \Elementor\Repeater();
$repeater->add_control( 'item_label', [...] );
$repeater->add_control( 'item_file', [...] );
$repeater->add_control( 'item_fallback_url', [...] );

$this->add_control( 'links', [
    'type'        => \Elementor\Controls_Manager::REPEATER,
    'fields'      => $repeater->get_controls(),
    'default'     => [
        [ 'item_label' => 'Português', 'item_file' => [ 'id' => 0, 'url' => '' ], 'item_fallback_url' => [ 'url' => '' ] ],
        [ 'item_label' => 'English', 'item_file' => [ 'id' => 0, 'url' => '' ], 'item_fallback_url' => [ 'url' => '' ] ],
    ],
] );
```

**JetEngine CCT Usage:**
- **participantes_cct** (CCT ID 70) stores 1,287 migrated participants in `wp_jet_cct_participantes_cct` table
- Script: **migrate-participantes-to-cct.php** (lines 56-100 show migration pattern using `$cct_module->manager->get_content_types()`)
- CCT fields: item_title, item_content, item_thumbnail, organizacao, link_de_acesso
- Indices created for performance: `idx_status_title`, `idx_item_title`, `idx_organizacao`

**Repeater Data Structure:**
Elementor stores repeater data as serialized arrays in postmeta `_elementor_data`. Example structure:
```
links => [
  {item_label: "Português", item_file: {id: 123, url: "..."}, item_fallback_url: {url: "..."}},
  {item_label: "English", item_file: {id: 456, url: "..."}, item_fallback_url: {url: "..."}}
]
```

## Exploration Complete - Comprehensive Report

I've thoroughly explored the codebase to understand how Elementor's MEDIA control handles non-image files. Here are my findings:

### 1. **Download Button Widget (JetElements)**
**File**: `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/plugins/jet-elements/includes/addons/jet-elements-download-button.php`

- Uses TEXT control for file ID, NOT MEDIA control with media type
- Line 72-80: `'download_file'` is a TEXT input accepting media library IDs
- Does NOT demonstrate media type filtering for PDFs/files

### 2. **Elementor Media Control Core**
**File**: `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/plugins/elementor/includes/controls/media.php`

**Key findings:**
- **Viewable media types** (lines 181-189): Only `'image'`, `'video'`, `'svg'` are considered "viewable"
- Non-viewable types (PDFs, audio, JSON, VTT, etc.) use a fallback UI without preview
- **Backward compatibility** (lines 171-177):
  - Converts singular `media_type` → `media_types` array
  - If SVG inline option enabled, adds `'svg'` to types
- **Default media_types** (line 435): `[ 'image' ]`
- **Preview types** (lines 192-202):
  - Video: shows `<video>` tag
  - Image/SVG: shows image preview
  - Everything else: shows file icon + filename

### 3. **Valid media_type Values (from codebase analysis)**

JetElements and Elementor use these media_type values:

| Control | File | media_type | Type |
|---------|------|-----------|------|
| Audio Player | `jet-elements-audio.php` | `'audio'` | String |
| Video Widget | `jet-elements-video.php` | `'video'` | String |
| Video Captions | `jet-elements-video.php` | `'text/vtt'` | MIME type |
| Inline SVG | `jet-elements-inline-svg.php` | `'svg'` | String |
| Lottie Animation | `jet-elements-lottie.php` | `'application/json'` | MIME type |
| Horizontal Timeline | `jet-elements-horizontal-timeline.php` | `'svg'` (array) | Array of strings |
| Link in Bio (Pro) | `widget-link-in-bio-base.php` | `[ 'application/pdf' ]` | Array of MIME types |
| Core Video Widget | `includes/widgets/video.php` | `[ 'video' ]` | Array of strings |

### 4. **Non-Viewable File UI Pattern**

When media_type is NOT in `['image', 'video', 'svg']`:
- **Lines 287-307** of `media.php`: Renders file picker UI
  - Shows "Click the media icon to upload file" text
  - Displays file icon (`eicon-document-file`)
  - Shows filename below icon
  - Upload/Remove buttons instead of preview

### 5. **URL Filtering Behavior**

The media library popup filters by MIME type via WordPress `media_frame.get()`. Examples:
- `'media_type': 'audio'` → filters to audio/*.* MIME types
- `'media_type': 'application/json'` → filters to application/json
- `'media_type': [ 'application/pdf' ]` → filters to application/pdf only
- `'media_type': 'video'` → filters to video/*.* MIME types

### 6. **Custom Elementor Mu-Plugin (Site-Level)**
**File**: `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-elementor-svg-widget.php`

- Custom widget `Bureau_SVG_Widget` for inline SVGs
- Uses SELECT control (not MEDIA) to pick SVGs from theme directory
- Does NOT extend or customize media control behavior
- Handles SVG styling with CSS custom properties

### 7. **Elementor Upload Manager**
**File**: `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/plugins/elementor/core/files/uploads-manager.php`

- Extends `upload_mimes` filter to support unfiltered uploads
- Registers file type handlers (SVG, JSON, etc.)
- Elementor allows uploads of ANY file type when unfiltered uploads enabled

---

## Summary

**How Elementor's MEDIA Control Works for Non-Image Files:**

1. **Parameters**: Use `media_type` OR `media_types` (with backward compatibility conversion)
2. **Viewable types**: Only `image`, `video`, `svg` show live previews
3. **Non-viewable types**: PDFs, JSON, audio, VTT, etc. show file picker UI instead
4. **Format**: Can be string (`'audio'`) or MIME type (`'application/json'`), or array of either
5. **No custom types**: Elementor doesn't support arbitrary custom media types—only MIME types WordPress recognizes

**For PDFs specifically**: Pass `'media_type' => [ 'application/pdf' ]` (as array) or `'media_type' => 'application/pdf'` (as string)

## Implementation Plan: BIT Dropdown Button v3.0.0 → v3.1.0

### Overview of changes

There are five independent feature groups plus a version bump. The work touches two PHP files and two CSS files (site copy + common mirror). The changes are additive — no existing controls are removed, no CSS rules are deleted, no HTML class names change (which protects any existing Elementor instances from losing their saved settings).

```

In both `bit-dropdown-btn.css` files, change the comment block line:
```

```
to:
```
 * Versão: 3.1.0
```

## Step 3 — Add item_file_size control to repeater

**Location:** inside `$repeater` definition, immediately after the existing `item_fallback_url` control (after line 94), before the `$this->add_control('links', ...)` call.

```php
$repeater->add_control( 'item_file_size', [
    'label'       => 'Tamanho do arquivo',
    'type'        => \Elementor\Controls_Manager::TEXT,
    'placeholder' => '3.2 MB',

No condition is needed here — it is always visible in the repeater item, and the render() will only emit the span when the value is non-empty.

## Step 6 — Refactor render()

The entire `render()` method is replaced. The logic is structured as two passes: first build the `$valid_links` array (resolving URLs regardless of source), then decide between single/dropdown mode.

Replace the existing `render()` method (lines 358–415) with:

```php
protected function render() {
    $s     = $this->get_settings_for_display();
    $label = esc_html( ! empty( $s['label'] ) ? $s['label'] : 'Download' );

    // ── Pass 1: Build $links array from selected data source ──────────────
    $links = [];

    if ( 'meta_field' === ( $s['data_source'] ?? 'static' ) && ! empty( $s['meta_key'] ) ) {
        // JetEngine meta repeater field source
        $raw = [];
        if ( function_exists( 'jet_engine' )
             && ! empty( jet_engine()->listings )
             && ! empty( jet_engine()->listings->data ) ) {
            $raw = jet_engine()->listings->data->get_meta( $s['meta_key'] );
        }
        if ( empty( $raw ) ) {
            $raw = get_post_meta( get_the_ID(), $s['meta_key'], true );
        }
        if ( is_string( $raw ) ) {
            $raw = maybe_unserialize( $raw );
        }
        $links_raw = is_array( $raw ) ? $raw : [];

        $k_label    = ! empty( $s['meta_key_label'] )        ? $s['meta_key_label']        : 'label';
        $k_file_id  = ! empty( $s['meta_key_file_id'] )      ? $s['meta_key_file_id']      : 'file_id';
        $k_fallback = ! empty( $s['meta_key_fallback_url'] )  ? $s['meta_key_fallback_url'] : 'fallback_url';
        $k_size     = ! empty( $s['meta_key_file_size'] )     ? $s['meta_key_file_size']    : 'file_size';

        foreach ( $links_raw as $row ) {
            $links[] = [
                'item_label'        => $row[ $k_label ]    ?? '',
                'item_file'         => [ 'id' => (int) ( $row[ $k_file_id ] ?? 0 ), 'url' => '' ],
                'item_fallback_url' => [ 'url' => $row[ $k_fallback ] ?? '', 'is_external' => false ],
                'item_file_size'    => $row[ $k_size ] ?? '',
            ];
        }
    } else {
        // Static Elementor repeater source
        $links = $s['links'] ?? [];
    }

    // ── Pass 2: Resolve each link to a valid href, filtering empties ──────
    $valid_links = [];
    foreach ( $links as $link ) {
        $attachment_id = (int) ( $link['item_file']['id'] ?? 0 );
        $fallback_url  = $link['item_fallback_url']['url'] ?? '';
        $is_external   = ! empty( $link['item_fallback_url']['is_external'] );

        if ( $attachment_id > 0
             && function_exists( 'jet_elements_download_handler' )
             && jet_elements_download_handler() instanceof Jet_Elements_Download_Handler ) {
            $href        = esc_url( jet_elements_download_handler()->get_download_link( $attachment_id ) );
            $target_attr = '';
        } elseif ( ! empty( $fallback_url ) ) {
            $href        = esc_url( $fallback_url );
            $target_attr = $is_external ? ' target="_blank"' : '';
        } else {
            continue; // No valid URL — skip item
        }

        $valid_links[] = [
            'label'       => esc_html( $link['item_label'] ?? '' ),
            'href'        => $href,
            'target_attr' => $target_attr,
            'file_size'   => esc_html( $link['item_file_size'] ?? '' ),
        ];
    }

    // ── Pass 3: Determine render mode and output HTML ─────────────────────
    $single_mode = ( count( $valid_links ) === 1 );

    // Icon HTML (shared between modes)
    $icon_settings = $s['icon'] ?? [];
    $icon_html     = '';
    if ( ! empty( $icon_settings['value'] ) ) {
        ob_start();
        \Elementor\Icons_Manager::render_icon( $icon_settings, [ 'aria-hidden' => 'true' ] );
        $icon_html = ob_get_clean();
    }
    $icon_output = ! empty( $icon_html ) ? $icon_html : $this->get_default_icon_svg(); // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.EscapeOutput.OutputNotEscaped

    echo '<div class="dropdown-btn-wrapper">';
    echo '<div class="dropdown-btn-container">';

    if ( $single_mode ) {
        // ── Single-item: render as direct <a> anchor ──────────────────────
        $item = $valid_links[0];
        echo '<a class="dropdown-btn-toggle dropdown-btn-single"'
           . ' href="' . $item['href'] . '"'
           . ' rel="nofollow noopener"'
           . $item['target_attr'] . '>';
        echo '<div class="dropdown-btn-content">';
        echo '<span class="dropdown-btn-label">' . $label . '</span>';
        if ( ! empty( $item['file_size'] ) ) {
            echo '<span class="dropdown-btn-file-size">' . $item['file_size'] . '</span>';
        }
        echo '<span class="dropdown-btn-icon">';
        echo $icon_output; // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.EscapeOutput.OutputNotEscaped
        echo '</span>';
        echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-content
        echo '</a>';
    } else {
        // ── Multi-item: render as button + dropdown ───────────────────────
        echo '<button class="dropdown-btn-toggle" type="button">';
        echo '<div class="dropdown-btn-content">';
        echo '<span class="dropdown-btn-label">' . $label . '</span>';
        echo '<span class="dropdown-btn-icon">';
        echo $icon_output; // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.EscapeOutput.OutputNotEscaped
        echo '</span>';
        echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-content
        echo '</button>';

        if ( ! empty( $valid_links ) ) {
            echo '<div class="dropdown-btn-menu">';
            foreach ( $valid_links as $item ) {
                echo '<a href="' . $item['href'] . '" rel="nofollow noopener"' . $item['target_attr'] . '>';
                echo '<span class="dropdown-btn-item-label">' . $item['label'] . '</span>';
                if ( ! empty( $item['file_size'] ) ) {
                    echo '<span class="dropdown-btn-file-size">' . $item['file_size'] . '</span>';
                }
                echo '</a>';
            }
            echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-menu
        }
    }

    echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-container
    echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-wrapper
}
```

**Key design decisions in this render():**

- The label text for dropdown items is now wrapped in `<span class="dropdown-btn-item-label">` rather than being a bare text node. This is a minor structural change needed to support flex layout alongside the file size span. It does not affect Elementor settings or CSS variables.
- `$valid_links` is built before the mode decision — the single/multi check uses the final resolved count, not the raw repeater count. This ensures a widget configured with two items where one has no valid URL is correctly treated as single-mode if only one resolves.
- `$single_mode` is false (uses button+dropdown) when `count($valid_links)` is 0 or >= 2. An empty widget renders a button with no dropdown, which is the existing behavior.

## Step 7 — CSS changes

### bit-dropdown-btn.css — additions to append at end of file

```css
/* ==========================================================================
   FILE SIZE LABEL (v3.1.0)
   ========================================================================== */

.dropdown-btn-container .dropdown-btn-file-size {
    font-size     : 0.75em;
    font-weight   : 400;
    text-transform: none;
    opacity       : 0.75;
}

/* Dropdown item layout — flex row when file size present */
.dropdown-btn-container .dropdown-btn-menu a {
    display        : flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items    : center;
    gap            : 8px;
}
```

Note: the `display: block` on `.dropdown-btn-menu a` at line 124 of the current CSS will be overridden by the new `display: flex` rule. Since both rules use the same selector (identical specificity), the new rule must appear after the existing one in the file — placing it at the end satisfies this.

Also update the header comment block version:
- Change `* Versão: 3.0.0` to `* Versão: 3.1.0`

## Step 8 — WPML documentation note (no code change)

WPML compatibility requires no code changes. Elementor's WPML Multilingual plugin (`wpml-elementor.php`) automatically handles translation of TEXT, URL, MEDIA, and REPEATER controls for any Elementor widget that uses standard control types. The widget already uses only standard controls. The new controls added in v3.1.0 (TEXT, SELECT, MEDIA with media_types) are all standard types and will be translated automatically.

The `meta_key` / `meta_key_*` controls are widget-level TEXT controls (not repeater sub-controls), so they will appear in WPML's "String Translation" interface if WPML string translation is active. No action needed.

     1→# Plano: Widget Elementor — BIT Dropdown Button
     2→
     3→## Contexto
     4→
     5→A página `/publicacoes/` usa botões "DOWNLOAD" com dropdown construídos manualmente via widget HTML do Elementor. O resultado é inconsistente: alguns itens do JetEngine listing têm a estrutura HTML completa (botão estilizado com borda, ícone, dropdown) enquanto outros exibem texto simples "DOWNLOADS" sem nenhum estilo. Converter o componente para um **widget Elementor nativo** resolve a inconsistência e permite controle visual direto no painel do Elementor.
     6→

     8→

    11→**`bit-dropdown-btn.php`** — apenas enfileira o CSS:

    13→add_action('wp_enqueue_scripts', fn() => wp_enqueue_style('bit-dropdown-btn', ...))
    14→```
    15→
    16→**`bit-dropdown-btn.css`** — estilos via CSS variables (`--btn-normal-*`).
    17→O ícone usa `mask-image: var(--ucpa-icon-download)` — variável **não definida** em `style.css` (é o motivo do ícone ausente/inconsistente em alguns botões).

    48→## Implementação
    49→
    50→### Arquivo 1 — `bit-dropdown-btn.php` (expandir)
    51→
    52→**Adicionar** classe `Bit_Dropdown_Btn_Widget` dentro de `elementor/widgets/register`, mantendo o `wp_enqueue_scripts` existente.
    53→
    54→**Controls — Tab Content:**
    55→
    56→| Controle | Tipo | Default |
    57→|---|---|---|
    58→| `label` | TEXT | `DOWNLOAD` |
    59→| `links` | REPEATER | — |
    60→| `links > item_label` | TEXT | `Português` |

    62→| `links > item_target` | SELECT | `_self` / `_blank` |

    64→**Controls — Tab Style:**
    65→
    66→Todos os controles usam `selectors` apontando para `{{WRAPPER}}` com CSS variables — o CSS file cuida do resto:
    67→
    68→| Controle | CSS variable |
    69→|---|---|
    70→| `btn_width` | SLIDER → `{{WRAPPER}} .dropdown-btn-toggle { width: {{SIZE}}{{UNIT}}; }` |
    71→| `color_bg` | COLOR → `{{WRAPPER}} { --btn-normal-bg: {{VALUE}}; }` |
    72→| `color_txt` | COLOR → `{{WRAPPER}} { --btn-normal-txt: {{VALUE}}; }` |
    73→| `color_bdr` | COLOR → `{{WRAPPER}} { --btn-normal-bdr: {{VALUE}}; }` |
    74→| `color_bg_hv` | COLOR → `{{WRAPPER}} { --btn-normal-bg-hv: {{VALUE}}; }` |
    75→| `color_txt_hv` | COLOR → `{{WRAPPER}} { --btn-normal-txt-hv: {{VALUE}}; }` |

    81→    $s = $this->get_settings_for_display();

'meta_field' ],\n    'ai'        => [ 'active' => false ],\n] );\n\n$this->add_control( 'meta_key_fallback_url', [\n    'label'     => 'Sub-campo: URL alternativa',\n    'type'      => 'meta_key_file_size', [\n    'label'     => 'Sub-campo: tamanho do arquivo',\n    'type'      => \\Elementor\\Controls_Manager::TEXT,\n    'default'   => 'file_size',\n    'condition' => [ Substituir método `render()` completo\n\n```php\nprotected function render() {\n    $s     = $this->get_settings_for_display();\n    $label = esc_html( ! empty( $s['label'] ) ? $s['label'] : 'Download' );\n\n    // ── Pass 1: Montar $links normalizado a partir da fonte selecionada ───\n    $links = [];\n\n    if ( 'meta_field' === ( $s['data_source'] ?? 'static' ) && ! empty( $s['meta_key'] ) ) {\n        $raw = [];\n        if ( function_exists( 'jet_engine' )\n             && ! empty( jet_engine()->listings )\n             && ! empty( jet_engine()->listings->data ) ) {\n            $raw = jet_engine()->listings->data->get_meta( $s['meta_key'] );\n        }\n        if ( empty( $raw ) ) {\n            $raw = get_post_meta( get_the_ID(), $s['meta_key'], true );\n        }\n        if ( is_string( $raw ) ) {\n            $raw = maybe_unserialize( $raw );\n        }\n\n        $k_label    = ! empty( $s['meta_key_label'] )        ? $s['meta_key_label']        : 'label';\n        $k_file_id  = ! empty( $s['meta_key_file_id'] )      ? $s['meta_key_file_id']      : 'file_id';\n        $k_fallback = ! empty( $s['meta_key_fallback_url'] )  ? $s['meta_key_fallback_url'] : 'fallback_url';\n        $k_size     = ! empty( $s['meta_key_file_size'] )     ? $s['meta_key_file_size']    : 'file_size';\n\n        foreach ( (array) $raw as $row ) {\n            $links[] = [\n                'item_label'        => $row[ $k_label ]    ?? '',\n                'item_file'         => [ 'id' => (int) ( $row[ $k_file_id ] ?? 0 ), 'url' => '' ],\n                'item_fallback_url' => [ 'url' => $row[ $k_fallback ] ?? '', 'is_external' => false ],\n                'item_file_size'    => $row[ $k_size ] ?? '',\n            ];\n        }\n    } else {\n        $links = $s['links'] ?? [];\n    }\n\n    // ── Pass 2: Resolver URLs, filtrar itens sem URL válida ───────────────\n    $valid_links = [];\n    foreach ( $links as $link ) {\n        $attachment_id = (int) ( $link['item_file']['id'] ?? 0 );\n        $fallback_url  = $link['item_fallback_url']['url'] ?? '';\n        $is_external   = ! empty( $link['item_fallback_url']['is_external'] );\n\n        if ( $attachment_id > 0\n             && function_exists( 'jet_elements_download_handler' )\n             && jet_elements_download_handler() instanceof Jet_Elements_Download_Handler ) {\n            $href        = esc_url( jet_elements_download_handler()->get_download_link( $attachment_id ) );\n            $target_attr = '';\n        } elseif ( ! empty( $fallback_url ) ) {\n            $href        = esc_url( $fallback_url );\n            $target_attr = $is_external ? ' target=\"_blank\"' : '';\n        } else {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        $valid_links[] = [\n            'label'       => esc_html( $link['item_label'] ?? '' ),\n            'href'        => $href,\n            'target_attr' => $target_attr,\n            'file_size'   => esc_html( $link['item_file_size'] ?? '' ),\n        ];\n    }\n\n    // ── Pass 3: Decidir modo e renderizar ─────────────────────────────────\n    $single_mode = ( count( $valid_links ) === 1 && 'yes' === ( $s['single_direct_download'] ?? 'yes' ) );\n\n    // Ícone (compartilhado entre modos)\n    $icon_settings = $s['icon'] ?? [];\n    $icon_html     = '';\n    if ( ! empty( $icon_settings['value'] ) ) {\n        ob_start();\n        \\Elementor\\Icons_Manager::render_icon( $icon_settings, [ 'aria-hidden' => 'true' ] );\n        $icon_html = ob_get_clean();\n    }\n    $icon_output = ! empty( $icon_html ) ? $icon_html : $this->get_default_icon_svg(); // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.EscapeOutput.OutputNotEscaped\n\n    echo '<div class=\"dropdown-btn-wrapper\">';\n    echo '<div class=\"dropdown-btn-container\">';\n\n    if ( $single_mode ) {\n        // Modo download direto — âncora sem dropdown\n        $item = $valid_links[0];\n        echo '<a class=\"dropdown-btn-toggle dropdown-btn-single\"'\n           . ' href=\"' . $item['href'] . '\"'\n           . ' rel=\"nofollow noopener\"'\n           . $item['target_attr'] . '>';\n        echo '<div class=\"dropdown-btn-content\">';\n        echo '<span class=\"dropdown-btn-label\">' . $label . '</span>';\n        if ( ! empty( $item['file_size'] ) ) {\n            echo '<span class=\"dropdown-btn-file-size\">' . $item['file_size'] . '</span>';\n        }\n        echo '<span class=\"dropdown-btn-icon\">';\n        echo $icon_output; // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.EscapeOutput.OutputNotEscaped\n        echo '</span>';\n        echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-content\n        echo '</a>';\n    } else {\n        // Modo dropdown\n        echo '<button class=\"dropdown-btn-toggle\" type=\"button\">';\n        echo '<div class=\"dropdown-btn-content\">';\n        echo '<span class=\"dropdown-btn-label\">' . $label . '</span>';\n        echo '<span class=\"dropdown-btn-icon\">';\n        echo $icon_output; // phpcs:ignore WordPress.Security.EscapeOutput.OutputNotEscaped\n        echo '</span>';\n        echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-content\n        echo '</button>';\n\n        if ( ! empty( $valid_links ) ) {\n            echo '<div class=\"dropdown-btn-menu\">';\n            foreach ( $valid_links as $item ) {\n                echo '<a href=\"' . $item['href'] . '\" rel=\"nofollow noopener\"' . $item['target_attr'] . '>';\n                echo '<span class=\"dropdown-btn-item-label\">' . $item['label'] . '</span>';\n                if ( ! empty( $item['file_size'] ) ) {\n                    echo '<span class=\"dropdown-btn-file-size\">' . $item['file_size'] . '</span>';\n                }\n                echo '</a>';\n            }\n            echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-menu\n        }\n    }\n\n    echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-container\n    echo '</div>'; // .dropdown-btn-wrapper\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Mudanças em `bit-dropdown-btn.css`\n\n### 1. Bump versão no comentário do topo: `3.0.0` → `3.1.0`\n\n### 2. `.dropdown-btn-menu a`: mudar `display: block` → `display: flex`\n\nLinha atual (124):\n```css\n.dropdown-btn-container .dropdown-btn-menu a {\n    display        : block;\n```\nMudar para:\n```css\n.dropdown-btn-container .dropdown-btn-menu a {\n    display        : flex;\n    justify-content: space-between;\n    align-items    : center;\n    gap            : 8px;\n```\n\n### 3. Acrescentar ao final do arquivo\n\n```css\n/* ==========================================================================\n   FILE SIZE LABEL (v3.1.0)\n   ========================================================================== */\n\n.dropdown-btn-container .dropdown-btn-file-size {\n    font-size     : 0.75em;\n    font-weight   : 400;\n    text-transform: none;\n    opacity       : 0.75;\n    white-space   : nowrap;\n    flex-shrink   : 0;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Mirror\n\nApós editar os arquivos do site, copiar para o mirror:\n\n```bash\ncp wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.php \\\n   /Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.php\n\ncp wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.css \\\n   /Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.css\n```\n\nCommitar em `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2` com:\n```\ngit add docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.php \\\n        docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.css\ngit commit -m \"feat(mu-plugins): BIT Dropdown Button v3.1.0 — file size, single toggle, JetEngine meta source\"\n```\n\nCommitar no repo do site (concertacao) com:\n```\ngit add wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.php \\\n        wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-dropdown-btn.css\ngit commit -m \"feat(dropdown-btn): v3.1.0 — file size, single toggle, JetEngine meta, media types fix\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Verificação\n\n1. `std all-cache-flush` — limpa OPcache e WP Rocket\n2. Elementor editor:\n   - \"Tamanho do arquivo\" aparece em cada item do repeater\n   - \"Download direto com 1 item\" switcher aparece no Content tab\n   - Picker de arquivo abre biblioteca sem filtro de imagem\n   - \"Fonte dos dados → Campo meta\" exibe 5 controles meta_key_*\n3. Frontend — 1 item + toggle ON: HTML tem `<a class=\"dropdown-btn-toggle dropdown-btn-single\">`, sem dropdown\n4. Frontend — 1 item + toggle OFF: HTML tem `<button>` + `.dropdown-btn-menu` (dropdown normal)\n5. Frontend — 2+ itens: sempre dropdown, file_size aparece alinhado à direita via flex\n6. Verificar `diff` entre site e mirror (saída deve ser vazia)\n"

   266→		/**
   267→		 * Process chuncked download
   268→		 *
   269→		 * @param  string $file     Filepath
   270→		 * @param  bool   $retbytes
   271→		 * @return mixed
   272→		 */
   273→		public function readfile_chunked( $filename, $retbytes = true ) {
   274→
   275→			$chunksize = 1 * ( 1024 * 1024 );
   276→			$buffer    = '';
   277→			$cnt       = 0;
   278→			$handle    = fopen( $filename, 'rb' );
   279→
   280→			if ( false === $handle ) {
   281→				return false;
   282→			}
   283→
   284→			while ( ! feof( $handle ) ) {
   285→				$buffer = fread( $handle, $chunksize );
   286→				echo $buffer; // phpcs:ignore
   287→
   288→				if ( ob_get_level() > 0 ) {
   289→					ob_flush();
   290→				}
   291→				flush();
   292→				if ( $retbytes ) {
   293→					$cnt += strlen( $buffer );
   294→				}
   295→			}
   296→
   297→			$status = fclose($handle);
   298→
   299→			if ( $retbytes && $status ) {
   300→				return $cnt; // return num. bytes delivered like readfile() does.
   301→			}
   302→
   303→			return $status;
   304→		}

   319→		}

> AGENT

Encontrei o problema. O `Jet_Elements_Download_Handler` tem um método público `get_file_size($id)` (linha 182). E o root cause da encriptação não funcionando: o controle MEDIA está restrito a imagens, então o usuário não consegue selecionar PDFs — acaba usando `item_fallback_url` com link direto, e aí `attachment_id = 0` → fallback é usado → sem encriptação.

O fix é duplo: corrigir `media_types` para PDFs + aproveitar `get_file_size()` para auto-detectar o tamanho quando não preenchido manualmente.

Vou atualizar o plano com isso:
